Abstract: The quantum kicked top (QKT) is one of the most widely studied models in quantum chaos, providing a minimal yet powerful framework for exploring the relationship between classical nonlinear dynamics and quantum behavior. Unlike many chaotic systems with infinite-dimensional Hilbert spaces, the QKT possesses a finite-dimensional Hilbert space, making it analytically and numerically controllable while still showing a rich dynamical phenomena. In this chapter, we present a comprehensive introduction to the QKT as a paradigmatic model of quantum chaos. Starting from the classical kicked top, we derive the discrete nonlinear map governing the dynamics on the unit sphere and analyze its phase space structure through fixed points, stability analysis, bifurcations and Lyapunov exponents. We then discuss the role of symmetries, including rotational and time-reversal symmetry, and how their breaking modifies the dynamics. The quantum description is developed using Floquet theory, where the periodically driven spin system is represented by a unitary Floquet operator acting on a $(2j+1)$-dimensional Hilbert space. Within this framework, signatures of quantum chaos such as spectral statistics, entanglement generation and recurrences are discussed. The model also admits an interpretation as a system of interacting qubits, enabling explicit few-qubit realizations and direct connections with quantum information measures through reduced density matrices and entanglement entropy. By linking classical phase space structures with quantum dynamical indicators, the QKT provides a clear setting to investigate the emergence of chaotic behavior in the semiclassical limit. The chapter, therefore, highlights the quantum kicked top as a bridge between nonlinear classical dynamics, quantum chaos and modern quantum information science.

</details>


### [122] [Chiral state conversion near an exceptional point: speed-noise competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12354)
*Qing-Wei Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了非厄米系统中环绕例外点时的动力学行为，特别关注噪声对动力学手性转换的影响，提出了定量测量手性的非手性度χ_c，并揭示了噪声与环绕速度之间的竞争关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米系统环绕例外点时会出现绝热定理破坏和手性态转换，但手性动力学对环路几何、起点、环绕速度特别是噪声的微妙依赖性尚未得到系统研究。需要定量分析噪声在非厄米动力学中的关键作用。

Method: 提出非手性度χ_c来定量测量手性；通过精确解分析无噪声动力学；通过数值积分分析有噪声动力学；使用一阶微扰理论和传递矩阵的条件数解释临界边界。

Result: 从破缺相开始的精确动力学显示手性振荡，在速度较小时对噪声极其敏感；环绕速度和噪声强度在决定χ_c时相互竞争，形成噪声极限和清洁极限两个显著极限；临界边界满足简单标度律。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了噪声在非厄米动力学中扮演的关键角色，发现噪声与环绕速度的竞争导致两种不同极限，临界边界可用一阶微扰理论解释，对理论和实验研究都具有重要意义。

Abstract: One intriguing property of non-Hermitian systems is the breakdown of adiabatic theorem and chiral state conversion as the system dynamically encircles exceptional points. However, the subtle dependence of the chiral dynamics on the loop geometry, the starting point, the encircling speed and especially the noise has not been studied systematically. Here we propose a non-chirality degree $χ_c$ to measure the chirality quantitatively and analyze it in dynamics without noise by exact solution and dynamics with noise by numerical integration. The exact dynamics starting from the broken phase show chirality oscillations, which are extremely sensitive to noise when the speed is small. The encircling speed and the noise strength are found to compete with each other in determining $χ_c$, resulting in two distinguished limits, namely the noisy limit and the clean limit. The critical boundary between the two limits satisfies a simple scaling law, which could be explained in terms of first-order perturbation theory and the condition number of the transfer matrix. Our findings reveal the essential role played by noise in non-Hermitian dynamics and are relevant for both theoretical and experimental investigations.

</details>


### [123] [Noise-Robust Ultrafast Entanglement Generation in Rydberg Atoms via Quantum Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12361)
*Tanveer Ahmad,Muhammad Muneer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文对两个里德堡阻塞原子间的超快纠缠生成进行了理论分析，考虑了实际激光噪声的影响，评估了不同噪声谱下贝尔态保真度的敏感性，并通过量子最优控制理论优化脉冲设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发超快中性原子量子处理器需要理解激光噪声对纠缠生成的影响，特别是里德堡阻塞原子间的超快纠缠操作，实际应用中激光噪声是主要限制因素。

Method: 使用飞秒高斯脉冲作为基准，通过蒙特卡洛系综模拟评估贝尔态保真度对幅度和相位噪声的敏感性，分析白噪声、粉红噪声（1/f）和Ornstein-Uhlenbeck噪声谱；应用量子最优控制理论（QOCT）和D-MORPH算法，在多个等式约束下优化脉冲设计。

Result: 幅度噪声容忍度较高，即使30%噪声水平下保真度仍超过90%；相位噪声是主要限制因素，超过约1%噪声幅度时保真度迅速下降；粉红噪声比相同幅度的白噪声造成更小的保真度损失；通过QOCT优化的双脉冲结构在无噪声情况下达到约99%保真度，在适度幅度噪声下保持高保真度，但存在约1%幅度噪声的崩溃阈值。

Conclusion: 该研究为飞秒级超快中性原子量子处理器的发展提供了实用基准，表明相位噪声是主要限制因素，而优化的脉冲设计可以在一定程度上缓解噪声影响，但存在明确的噪声容忍阈值。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive theoretical analysis of ultrafast entanglement generation between two Rydberg-blockaded atoms, explicitly accounting for realistic laser noise. Using femtosecond Gaussian pulses as a baseline, we systematically evaluate Bell-state fidelity sensitivity to amplitude and phase noise across white, pink (1/f), and Ornstein-Uhlenbeck spectra using Monte Carlo ensemble simulations. Our results show that amplitude noise is well tolerated, with fidelities above 90% even at 30% noise levels, while phase noise is the primary limiting factor, causing fidelity to drop rapidly beyond about 1% noise amplitude. The spectral structure of the noise is also important: pink noise consistently causes less fidelity loss than white noise of the same amplitude. By applying quantum optimal control theory (QOCT) with the D-MORPH algorithm under multiple equality constraints, we obtain a double-pulse structure with a spectral notch that achieves approximately 99% fidelity in the noise-free case and maintains high fidelity under moderate amplitude noise. A breakdown threshold near 1% amplitude noise is identified, beyond which even optimized pulses cannot sustain coherent control. These results offer practical benchmarks for the development of ultrafast neutral-atom quantum processors operating in the femtosecond regime.

</details>


### [124] [A Periodic Orbit Trace Formula for Quantum Scrambling: The Role of the Normally Hyperbolic Invariant Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12369)
*Stephen Wiggins*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文推导了在具有指数-1鞍点的系统中，局域微正则OTOC的领先阶半经典展开，将量子信息混乱速率表达为NHIM上不稳定周期轨道的相干和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究OTOCs与局域相空间结构（如化学过渡态）之间的理论联系，需要形式化的发展。当前缺乏将量子信息混乱与经典相空间不稳定性联系起来的系统理论框架。

Method: 使用过渡态的正规形式理论，将鞍点附近的哈密顿量变换为可积形式。推导微正则迹、可积系统的半经典传播子、稳定性矩阵的因式分解，以及平稳相位近似的Schur补约化。

Result: 得到了局域微正则OTOC的半经典展开，将混乱速率表达为NHIM上不稳定周期轨道的相干和，推导出控制领先阶半经典增长窗口的局域不稳定性指数Λ(J)。在特殊情况下，迹和简化为有效的1.5Λ标度。

Conclusion: 该工作将周期轨道迹方法扩展到混乱可观测量，建立了量子信息混乱与经典相空间不稳定性的直接联系。横向作用量对不稳定性的依赖为模式选择性控制混乱提供了理论机制，并提出了数值评估策略来验证预测。

Abstract: Out-of-Time-Order Correlators (OTOCs) quantify quantum information scrambling, but their connection to localized phase-space structures, such as chemical transition states, requires formal development. We derive a leading-order semiclassical expansion for the local microcanonical OTOC in systems with an index-1 saddle point, expressing the scrambling rate as a coherent sum over unstable periodic orbits on the Normally Hyperbolic Invariant Manifold (NHIM). Valid in the semiclassical limit and the intermediate-time regime before the Ehrenfest time, our derivation utilizes the Normal Form theory of the transition state, which transforms the Hamiltonian near the saddle into an integrable (though generally non-separable) form dependent on conserved actions. We outline the derivation of the microcanonical trace, the semiclassical propagator for integrable systems, the factorization of the stability matrix, and the Schur complement reduction of the stationary phase approximation. Our result extends periodic-orbit trace methods to scrambling observables, yielding a local instability exponent Λ(J) governing the leading semiclassical growth window. As a special case, when the observation time coincides with the intrinsic periods of the contributing orbits, the trace sum reduces to an effective 1.5Λ scaling, resulting from the competition between local hyperbolic growth and wavepacket dilution. This simplified form is conditional; the full expansion retains a coherent sum over orbit periods. Finally, we discuss how the dependence of the instability on transverse actions establishes a theoretical mechanism for mode-selective control of scrambling, and outline a numerical evaluation strategy to test these predictions.

</details>


### [125] [Enhanced quantum illumination of a lossy target: A sequential interaction model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12381)
*Shilpi Srivastava,Shubhrangshu Dasgupta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在噪声环境和目标温度不同的现实条件下，量子照明对损耗目标的检测性能，比较了双模压缩态与经典相干态协议的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子照明在现实环境中的有效性，考虑目标与周围环境温度不同的情况，以及信号与噪声环境和目标的顺序相互作用，旨在改进量子雷达和激光雷达系统中的目标检测。

Method: 采用独立分束器模型模拟信号与噪声环境及目标的相互作用，使用信噪比和量子切尔诺夫界量化检测性能，比较高斯双模压缩态与基于相干态的最优经典协议。

Result: 对于低反射率目标和任意相位变化，双模压缩态始终获得比相干态更高的信噪比，且对热噪声具有鲁棒性；同时获得了比先前报道结果更低的量子切尔诺夫界，表明目标存在与否的可区分性更强。

Conclusion: 现实建模在改进基于量子照明的损耗目标检测中发挥关键作用，双模压缩态在低反射率目标检测中优于经典协议，对量子雷达和激光雷达系统具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: The effectiveness of quantum illumination (QI) of a lossy target is investigated in a realistic setting in which the signal sequentially interacts with a noisy environment and the target. The target is considered at a temperature distinct from its surroundings, while both the interactions are modeled as an action of independent beam splitters with different reflectivities. The detection performance is quantified using the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) and the quantum Chernoff bound (QCB), the latter providing an upper bound on the error probability. The performance of the Gaussian two-mode squeezed state (TMSS) is compared with that of the optimal classical protocol based on coherent states (CS). The proposed model shows that TMSS consistently achieves a higher SNR than CS for a low-reflectivity target and an arbitrary phase change and remains robust against thermal noise. Furthermore, a sufficiently lower QCB is obtained for TMSS than in previously reported results, indicating greater distinguishability between the presence and absence of the target. These findings underscore the role of realistic modeling in improving QI-based detection of lossy targets, with potential relevance to quantum radar and lidar systems.

</details>


### [126] [Permutationally symmetric molecular aggregates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12395)
*Sricharan Raghavan-Chitra,Arghadip Koner,Joel Yuen-Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了分子聚集体线性光谱的经典光学方法（DDA/CES/CPA）的精确性极限，发现在全对全耦合的置换对称聚集体中，当N→∞时这些方法是精确的，并建立了1/N展开来修正有限N的量子效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子聚集体线性光谱通常用经典光学方法（如DDA、CES、CPA）近似，但这些方法的有效性极限不明确。需要从量子力学角度确定这些经典方法的精确性条件及其修正。

Method: 从量子力学哈密顿量出发，研究全对全耦合的置换对称聚集体（分子版Lipkin-Meshkov-Glick模型）。借鉴分子极化子问题的技术，建立1/N展开来修正经典光学极限，并通过同二聚体的计算验证。

Result: 发现当N→∞时，经典光学方法（DDA/CPA/CES）在全对全耦合置换对称聚集体中是精确的。有限N修正表现为类似拉曼的单体跃迁。同二聚体计算验证了这些量子修正的存在。

Conclusion: 经典光学方法在特定极限下是精确的，但有限尺寸的量子聚集体表现出超越经典光学的量子光学特征。这阐明了简单量子发射体阵列中量子效应的存在。

Abstract: Linear optical spectra of molecular aggregates are often approximated by classical optics methods such as the discrete-dipole approximation (DDA), coherent exciton scattering (CES), and coherent potential approximation (CPA), where the only quantum-mechanical input to the calculation is the linear susceptibility of the monomers. However, the limits of validity of these classical optics methods remain opaque. Here, starting from a quantum mechanical Hamiltonian for the aggregate, we identify a limit where DDA/CPA/CES is exact: all-to-all coupled permutationally symmetric aggregates of $N \to \infty$ monomers. The permutational symmetry of this molecular version of the Lipkin-Meshkov-Glick model, which is closely related to that of the molecular polariton problem of many identical molecules coupled to a single-cavity mode, allows us to borrow recent techniques developed for the latter. In particular, we identify a $1/N$ expansion that corrects the classical optics limit with finite $N$ corrections to the linear response of the aggregate. These corrections feature as Raman-like transitions of a single monomer. We illustrate these findings with calculations on the very physically-relevant setup of a homodimer. Our findings clarify how quantum optical features that go beyond classical optics can already be present in simple arrays of quantum emitters such as molecular aggregates.

</details>


### [127] [Efficient classical training of model-free quantum photonic reservoir](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12441)
*Rosario Di Bartolo,Valeria Cimini,Giorgio Minati,Danilo Zia,Luca Innocenti,Salvatore Lorenzo,Gabriele Lo Monaco,Nicolò Spagnolo,Taira Giordani,G. Massimo Palma,Mauro Paternostro,Alessandro Ferraro,Fabio Sciarrino*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种光子量子极限学习机的训练策略，其中学习和测量设置优化完全使用经典光完成，而推理则在真正的量子态上进行，实现了经典到量子的知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子技术中，由于实验缺陷、漂移和量子动力学模型不精确，模型无关的量子态性质估计面临挑战。需要开发不依赖设备先验模型的训练方法。

Method: 基于相干态通过线性光学储层演化后的归一化输出强度与可分离输入量子态输出统计之间的恒等关系，直接在实验数据上实现模型无关、基于梯度的储层测量投影优化。

Result: 实验证明该方法能准确重建未见单光子态的单量子比特泡利可观测量，并扩展到任意二分态的双量子比特纠缠见证估计，实现了跨经典-量子边界的分布外泛化。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了光子量子学习设备快速、自适应和资源高效训练的实际途径，实现了经典到量子边界的独特泛化形式。

Abstract: Model-independent estimation of the properties of quantum states is a central challenge in quantum technologies, as experimental imperfections, drifts, and imprecise models of the actual quantum dynamics inevitably hinder accurate reconstructions. Here, we introduce a training strategy for photonic quantum extreme learning machines in which both the learning stage and the optimization of the measurement settings are performed entirely with classical light, while inference is carried out on genuinely quantum states. The protocol is based on the identity between the normalized output intensities following the evolution of coherent states through a linear optical reservoir, and the output statistics obtained with separable input quantum states. Building on this correspondence, we implemented a model-free, gradient-based optimization of the reservoir measurement projection directly on experimental data, without relying on a prior model of the device transformation. We experimentally show that the resulting classical-to-quantum transfer enables accurate reconstruction of single-qubit Pauli observables for previously unseen single-photon states, and extends to the estimation of a two-qubit entanglement witness for arbitrary bipartite states. Beyond demonstrating a qualitatively distinct form of out-of-distribution generalization across the classical-to-quantum boundary, our results identify a practical route to fast, adaptive, and resource-efficient training of photonic quantum learning devices.

</details>


### [128] [$\mathbb{Z}_{2}$ Skin Channels and Scale-Dependent Dynamical Quantum Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12450)
*Yongxu Fu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文通过结合半经典世界线视角与对趋肤效应的深入理解，解析描述了具有反常时间反演对称性的非厄米系统中周期性边界条件下的动态分离Z₂趋肤通道。这些通道表现出动量空间中的指数主导时间演化，实空间中沿一维链循环，导致量子复兴和动态量子相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于深入理解非厄米系统中反常时间反演对称性下的动态趋肤效应，特别是周期性边界条件下波包演化的分离通道现象。作者旨在结合半经典世界线视角与趋肤效应的增强理解，解析描述这些通道的动力学行为，并验证先前关于世界线缠绕和缠绕控制机制的发现。

Method: 采用解析方法，结合半经典世界线视角与对趋肤效应的增强理解。通过分析具有反常时间反演对称性的非厄米系统，在周期性边界条件下研究动态分离的Z₂趋肤通道。重点关注波包在动量空间中的指数主导时间演化及其在实空间中的循环行为。

Result: 研究发现：1）动态分离的Z₂趋肤通道在动量空间中表现出指数主导的时间演化，其振幅最大值向主导动量演化；2）在实空间中，这些通道沿一维链循环，形成半经典世界线；3）这种循环导致量子复兴和动态量子相变，且后者表现出尺度依赖行为，与传统动态量子相变不同；4）研究严格证明了先前关于世界线缠绕和缠绕控制机制的发现，确认核心物理与普通趋肤效应共享。

Conclusion: 该工作通过解析方法成功描述了非厄米系统中反常时间反演对称性下的动态分离Z₂趋肤通道。这些通道的循环行为导致量子复兴和尺度依赖的动态量子相变，验证了世界线缠绕机制的核心物理与普通趋肤效应的一致性，为非厄米系统的动力学研究提供了新视角。

Abstract: We analytically describe the dynamically separated $\mathbb{Z}_{2}$ skin channels (wavepacket evolutions) under periodic boundary condition (PBC) in non-Hermitian systems with anomalous time-reversal symmetry (ATRS), by combining the semiclassical worldline perspective with an enhanced understanding of skin effects. These channels, tied to the initial state and relevant symmetries, exhibit individually exponential-dominated time evolution in momentum space, where their amplitude maxima evolve toward the dominant momenta. In real space, they circulate around the one-dimensional (1D) chain, tracing semiclassical worldlines. Such circulations imply quantum revivals and dynamical quantum phase transitions (DQPTs) regardless of any wavepackets' phase interference, with the latter showing scale-dependent behavior, a feature distinct from conventional DQPTs. This work rigorously demonstrates our previous findings on worldline windings and the winding-control mechanism, confirming that the core physics is shared with the ordinary skin effect.

</details>


### [129] [Many-body localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12464)
*Jakub Zakrzewski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 对多体量子系统中非遍历动力学和MBL现象的介绍性综述


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 介绍和综述多体量子系统中的非遍历动力学现象，特别是多体局域化，为理解量子系统从遍历到局域化的转变提供理论基础

Method: 以XXZ模型为范例，分析有限系统中从遍历到MBL的交叉现象，并扩展到其他模型展示该现象的普遍性

Result: 总结了多体局域化的证据和特征，展示了从遍历到局域化转变的普遍性，并探讨了MBL与量子计算的关系

Conclusion: 多体局域化是量子多体系统中普遍存在的非遍历现象，与量子计算有重要但尚未充分探索的联系

Abstract: We present an introductory review of nonergodic dynamics in interacting many-body quantum systems, focusing on the phenomenon of many-body localization (MBL). We describe aspects of MBL and summarize the evidence for a crossover from the ergodic to the MBL regime in finite systems, using the paradigmatic XXZ model as an example. We then broaden the scope to other models to illustrate the generality of the phenomenon. We briefly touch on the largely unexplored relation between MBL and quantum computing.

</details>


### [130] [Demonstrating Record Fidelity for the Quantum Fourier Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12465)
*Philipp Aumann,Michael Fellner,David Alber,Max Cykiert,Christoph Fleckenstein,Roeland ter Hoeven,Leo Stenzel,Riccardo J. Valencia-Tortora,Wolfgang Lechner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在量子硬件上演示Parity架构，使用量子傅里叶变换作为基准测试，在IBM Heron r3芯片上实现了50量子位的记录性能，保真度约10^{-2}，相比之前交换方法具有超指数级加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 展示Parity架构在量子硬件上的实际性能，验证其在处理量子傅里叶变换等算法时的优越性，特别是在保真度和量子比特数量方面的突破。

Method: 采用基于原生CZ门指令集的量子处理器，在IBM Heron r3芯片上实现Parity架构，使用量子傅里叶变换作为基准算法进行测试，并探索包含iSWAP门的指令集改进方案。

Result: 在50量子位规模下实现了约10^{-2}的算法过程保真度，创造了保真度和量子比特数量的记录；相比之前的交换方法实现了超指数级加速O(exp(N^2))；进一步研究表明包含iSWAP门可以改善缩放性能。

Conclusion: Parity架构在量子硬件上表现出卓越性能，特别是在大规模量子比特系统中实现了前所未有的保真度和加速效果，为量子计算的实际应用提供了重要进展。

Abstract: We demonstrate the Parity Architecture on quantum hardware, using the quantum Fourier transform (QFT) as a benchmark. As a result, a record performance in both fidelity and qubit count is achieved using quantum processors with a native CZ-based instruction set. On the IBM Heron r3 chip, a process fidelity of the QFT algorithm of ${F \approx 10^{-2}}$ for ${N=50}$ qubits is achieved. The scaling of the speedup compared to previous swap-based methods is super-exponential $\mathcal{O}(\exp(N^2))$. Furthermore, we show that the scaling can be improved further by including iSWAP gates in the instruction set.

</details>


### [131] [Noise-enhanced quantum kernels on analog quantum computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12476)
*Hsiang-Wei Huang,Shen-Liang Yang,Chuan-Chi Huang,Yueh-Nan Chen,Hong-Bin Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究构建了模拟量子核和混合量子核，在基准测试和非马尔可夫性估计任务中表现出竞争力，并发现操作噪声能提升量子核性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子核方法作为有前景的量子机器学习算法，具有展示量子优势的潜力。当前大多数量子核基于门电路构建，本研究受模拟量子计算启发，探索构建模拟量子核和混合量子核，并研究操作噪声对量子核性能的影响。

Method: 1. 构建模拟量子核和混合量子核（不同于传统的门电路量子核）；2. 在基准测试任务中评估其性能；3. 应用于从稀疏数据估计非马尔可夫性的实际问题；4. 将操作噪声纳入量子核中，研究噪声对性能的影响。

Result: 1. 模拟量子核和混合量子核在基准测试中与其他核方法相比具有竞争力；2. 在从稀疏数据估计非马尔可夫性的实际问题上表现良好；3. 操作噪声的存在能提升量子核的性能；4. 噪声增强性能归因于噪声提高了表达能力和模型复杂度。

Conclusion: 该研究为量子核方法的实际实现开辟了新途径，并为减少实验需求下高效估计非马尔可夫性提供了有效方法。反直觉的噪声增强性能现象揭示了噪声在提升量子核表达能力和模型复杂度方面的积极作用。

Abstract: The quantum kernel method, a promising quantum machine learning algorithm, possesses substantial potential for demonstrating quantum advantage. Although the majority of the quantum kernel is constructed in the context of gate-based quantum circuits, inspired by the idea of analog quantum computing, here we construct an analog quantum kernel and a hybrid quantum kernel, and show their competitiveness against other kernel methods in a benchmarking task and the practical problem of estimating non-Markovianity from sparse data. Additionally, we also incorporate operational noise into the quantum kernels. Our results reveal that the presence of operational noise can be beneficial to the performance of the developed quantum kernels. We attribute this counterintuitive noise-enhanced performance to the improved expressivity and higher model complexity induced by noise. These results pave the way for practical implementations of quantum kernel methods and provide an efficient approach for estimating non-Markovianity with reduced experimental demands.

</details>


### [132] [Scattering Faddeev calculations in the double continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12516)
*Romain Guérout*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究利用位形空间Faddeev形式体系研究三粒子在双连续谱中的散射，其中所有粒子都是自由的，并将所有散射过程收集到单一矩阵中，应用于中子-氘核散射基准系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三粒子在双连续谱中的散射过程，其中所有粒子都处于自由状态，需要开发一个统一的理论框架来描述从单连续谱和双连续谱开始和结束的所有散射过程。

Method: 采用位形空间Faddeev形式体系，构建一个包含所有散射过程的单一矩阵，该方法应用于中子-氘核散射这一基准系统。

Result: 成功建立了描述三粒子在双连续谱中散射的统一理论框架，能够处理从单连续谱和双连续谱开始和结束的所有散射过程。

Conclusion: 位形空间Faddeev形式体系为研究三粒子在双连续谱中的散射提供了有效的理论工具，特别适用于中子-氘核散射等基准系统。

Abstract: We use the configuration-space Faddeev formalism to study scattering of three particles in the double continuum where all particles are free. All scattering processes, starting from and ending in both single and double continua, are collected in a unique matrix. We apply our method to the benchmark system of neutron-deuteron scattering.

</details>


### [133] [Utility of NISQ devices: optimizing experimental parameters for the fabrication of Au atomic junction using gate-based quantum computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12549)
*Takumi Kanezashi,Daisuke Tsukayama,Jun-ichi Shirakashi,Tetsuo Shibuya,Hiroshi Imai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究比较了NISQ设备与D-Wave量子退火机在反馈控制电迁移参数优化中的性能，发现NISQ设备在解决大规模组合优化问题时具有更低的残余能量和更高质量的近似解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反馈控制电迁移需要精细调节多个实验参数，手动调参存在显著挑战。本研究旨在探索利用门基量子计算在NISQ设备上自主制造金原子结的可行性，并与现有量子退火系统进行性能比较。

Method: 使用噪声中等规模量子（NISQ）设备近似求解组合优化问题，实现反馈控制电迁移参数的自主优化。将NISQ设备的计算精度与先前报道的D-Wave量子退火机进行对比分析。

Result: NISQ设备在解决大规模组合优化问题时表现出更低的残余能量，相比量子退火系统能产生更高质量的近似解。

Conclusion: NISQ设备在反馈控制电迁移参数优化方面具有优势，为自主制造原子结提供了可行的量子计算方法，性能优于现有的量子退火系统。

Abstract: Feedback-controlled electromigration (FCE) enables precise regulation of atomic migration by carefully optimizing multiple experimental parameters. However, manually fine-tuning these parameters poses significant challenges. This study investigated the feasibility of autonomously fabricating Au atomic junctions through gate-based quantum computing using a noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) device, which effectively approximates solutions to combinatorial optimization problems. We compared the computational accuracy of the NISQ device against a previously reported D-Wave quantum annealer. The results indicate that the NISQ device achieved lower residual energies and produced higher-quality approximate solutions for large-scale problems than the quantum annealing system.

</details>


### [134] [Design automation and space-time reduction for surface-code logical operations using a SAT-based EDA kernel compatible with general encodings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12560)
*Wang Liao,Rei Tokami,Yasunari Suzuki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: KOVAL-Q：基于SAT的表面码逻辑操作验证与优化EDA内核，支持灵活编码和高级布局，可确定最小执行时间并减少FTQC应用约10%执行时间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算机（FTQC）基于表面码和晶格手术已广泛研究，需要类似经典电路设计EDA的框架来识别低时空成本的逻辑操作、验证其功能和容错性，并在给定搜索空间中证明其最优性

Method: 提出KOVAL-Q EDA内核，将表面码逻辑操作验证和优化问题表述为可满足性（SAT）问题。相比现有LaSsynth等SAT框架，能处理更灵活表面码编码的逻辑量子比特（作为目标配置和中间状态），支持快速块等高级布局优化。包含三个对应不同类型约束的子内核

Result: KOVAL-Q能确定给定空间布局中基本逻辑操作的最小执行时间（如d周期逻辑CNOT和2d周期补丁旋转）。在简化调度模型下，可减少广泛研究的FTQC应用约10%执行时间。子内核设计便于集成到可扩展启发式框架中

Conclusion: KOVAL-Q为优化和验证核心FTQC子程序提供了必要框架，支持灵活编码、扩展搜索空间，并能集成到更大规模的设计流程中

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computers (FTQCs) based on surface codes and lattice surgery have been widely studied, and there is strong demand for a framework that can identify logical operations with low space-time cost, verify their functionality and fault tolerance, and demonstrate their optimality within a given search space, much like electronic design automation (EDA) in classical circuit design. In this paper, we propose KOVAL-Q, an EDA kernel that verifies and optimizes surface-code logical operations by formulating them as a satisfiability (SAT) problem. Compared with existing SAT-based frameworks such as LaSsynth, our method can handle logical qubits with more flexible surface-code encodings, both as target configurations and as intermediate states. This extension enables the optimization of advanced layouts, such as fast blocks, and broadens the search space for logical operations. We demonstrate that KOVAL-Q can determine the minimum execution time of fundamental logical operations in given spatial layouts, such as $d$-cycle logical CNOTs and $2d$-cycle patch rotations. Their use reduces the execution time of widely studied FTQC applications by about 10% under a simplified scheduling model. KOVAL-Q consists of three subkernels corresponding to different types of constraints, which facilitates its integration as a submodule into scalable heuristic frameworks. Thus, our proposal provides an essential framework for optimizing and validating core FTQC subroutines.

</details>


### [135] [Detecting entanglement from few partial transpose moments and their decay via weight enumerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12576)
*Daniel Miller,Jens Eisert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于三个PT矩的纠缠判据，相比传统方法显著降低了实验开销，并在特定条件下证明了高阶矩判据与PPT判据的等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于PT矩的纠缠判据通常需要所有k≤m阶矩，实验成本高。需要开发更高效的判据以减少实验开销，同时保持或提升检测能力。

Method: 提出基于任意三个PT矩(k<l<m)的纠缠判据：若p_l > p_k^x p_m^{1-x}（其中x=(m-l)/(m-k)），则状态必定纠缠。研究了局部和全局退极化GHZ态，比较了多种纠缠判据性能。引入量子权重枚举器概念分析局部白噪声下PT矩衰减。

Result: 1) 三矩判据显著降低实验开销；2) 对于全局退极化稳定子态，k≤5阶矩足以重现完整PPT判据；3) 当ρ^Γ特征值不超过(m+1)/2个时，Stieltjes-m判据与PPT判据等价；4) 量子权重枚举器可描述任意量子态在局部白噪声下PT矩衰减。

Conclusion: 三矩PT判据提供了实验上更可行的纠缠检测方案，在特定条件下与标准PPT判据等价。量子权重枚举器为分析噪声下纠缠特性提供了新工具，推动了高阶矩PPT松弛方法的发展及其在量子纠错和信息理论中的应用。

Abstract: The $p_3$-PPT criterion is an experimentally viable relaxation of the well-known positive partial transposition (PPT) criterion for the certification of quantum entanglement. Recently, it has been generalized to various families of entanglement criteria based on the PT moments $p_k=$Tr$[(ρ^Γ)^k]$, where $ρ^Γ$ denotes the partially transposed density matrix of a quantum state $ρ$. While most of these generalizations are strictly more powerful than the $p_3$-PPT criterion, their $m$-th level versions usually rely on the availability of $p_k$ for all moment orders $k\le m$. Here, we show that one can alternatively compare any three PT moments of orders $k<l<m$, which can significantly reduce experimental overheads. More precisely, we show that any state satisfying $p_l>p_k^xp_m^{1-x} $ must be entangled, where $x=(m-l)/(m-k)$. Using the example of locally depolarized GHZ states, we identify the most promising versions of these three-moment criteria and compare their performance with a broad range of entanglement criteria. In the case of globally depolarized stabilizer states, we prove that having access to $p_k$ for $k \le 5$ is sufficient to reproduce the full PPT criterion. More generally, we show that the Stieltjes-$m$ criterion is as powerful as the PPT criterion whenever $ρ^Γ$ has no more than $(m+1)/2$ distinct eigenvalues. Finally, we introduce a notion of quantum weight enumerators that capture the decay of $p_k$ under local white noise for arbitrary quantum states and illustrate this concept for an AME state. Our results contribute to the growing body of literature on higher-moment PPT relaxations and modern applications of weight enumerators in quantum error correction and information theory.

</details>


### [136] [Ternary Quantum Eraser Cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12577)
*Ahmed Halawani,Yahya Meshalwi Khabrani,Abdulaziz Al-Mogheeth,Zheng-Hong Li,M. Al-Amri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文分析了基于量子擦除的量子密钥分发协议，发现二进制实现存在85%的安全漏洞，并提出了一种采用三个120°偏振态和三光子随机时序的三进制量子擦除协议，将窃听者最大成功概率降至54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于量子擦除现象的量子密钥分发协议具有操作优势：通过干涉自动识别匹配和不匹配的编码选择，无需公开信道进行基比对。然而，安全分析显示二进制量子擦除实现允许窃听者以85%概率正确识别传输的量子态，这种漏洞无论采用何种状态随机化方案都存在。

Method: 引入三进制量子擦除协议：使用三个偏振态（120°角间隔），以三光子组形式传输，并采用随机化时序排序。该协议通过两种互补机制增强安全性：1）对称排列量子态的可区分性降低限制了单光子识别；2）未知光子排序的组合复杂性限制了多光子窃听策略。在四维路径-偏振希尔伯特空间中对个体窃听攻击进行安全分析。

Result: 安全分析表明，窃听者的最大成功概率被限制在54%，显著低于二进制区分界限（85%）。该协议保持了每光子0.30比特的二进制等效效率，与已建立的QKD实现具有竞争力，同时保留了量子擦除密码学固有的操作简单性。

Conclusion: 二进制量子擦除协议存在85%概率的安全漏洞，这反映了所有二态量子密码协议的基本限制，源于非正交态区分的几何结构。通过扩展到三进制协议，利用对称态排列和随机光子排序的组合复杂性，可以显著增强安全性，将窃听者成功概率降至54%，同时保持操作效率和简单性。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution protocols based on the quantum eraser phenomenon offer an operational advantage: automatic identification of matching and mismatching encoding choices through interference, eliminating basis reconciliation over public channels. However, security analysis reveals that binary quantum eraser implementations permit an eavesdropper to correctly identify transmitted quantum states with 85\% probability using optimal measurement strategies. This vulnerability persists regardless of state randomization schemes. We demonstrate that this limitation reflects a fundamental bound on all two-state quantum cryptographic protocols, arising from the geometry of non-orthogonal state discrimination. To overcome this constraint, we introduce a ternary quantum eraser protocol employing three polarization states with $120^\circ$ angular separation, transmitted in three-photon groups with randomized temporal ordering. This extension achieves enhanced security through two complementary mechanisms. First, the reduced distinguishability of symmetrically-arranged quantum states limits single-photon discrimination. Second, the combinatorial complexity of unknown photon ordering constrains multi-photon eavesdropping strategies. Security analysis against individual eavesdropping attacks within the four-dimensional path-polarization Hilbert space establishes that an eavesdropper's maximum success probability is bounded at 54\% substantially below the binary discrimination bound. The protocol maintains a binary-equivalent efficiency of 0.30 bits per photon competitive with established QKD implementations while preserving the operational simplicity inherent to quantum eraser cryptography.

</details>


### [137] [Mutual information harvesting for circularly accelerated detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12629)
*Mingkun Quan,Runhu Li,Zixu Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究两个圆形加速探测器在反射边界附近与无质量标量场相互作用时的互信息获取，发现互信息随探测器间距、加速度和半径变化呈现振荡行为，边界效应增强振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在反射边界存在的情况下，两个圆形加速探测器如何从量子场中获取互信息，探索加速度、边界效应和探测器几何配置对量子信息交换的影响。

Method: 采用两个具有相同加速度和轨迹半径的圆形加速探测器模型，探测器共享同一旋转轴，与无质量标量场相互作用，分析互信息随探测器间距、加速度、半径和能量间隙的变化规律。

Result: 互信息随探测器间距增加呈现振荡行为；加速度增大导致互信息峰值增大；边界附近互信息振荡且可达最大值；小间距时互信息随加速度先增后减；中等间距时互信息随加速度振荡；大加速度小半径（快速旋转）时互信息随能量间隙先减后振荡最终归零。

Conclusion: 快速旋转（大加速度小半径）显著改变场的真空涨落，导致互信息振荡行为；边界附近振荡增强表明这与边界反射的相干叠加相关，揭示了加速度和边界效应对量子信息交换的协同影响。

Abstract: We investigate the mutual information harvesting of two circularly accelerated detectors that interact with the massless scalar fields near a reflecting boundary. We consider that the two detectors share a common rotational axis with the same acceleration and trajectory radius. As the interdetector separation increases, the mutual information may exhibit oscillatory behavior at large acceleration and small radius. For a fixed radius, a larger acceleration leads to a larger peak value of the mutual information. Near the boundary, the mutual information may oscillate and the maximum can be obtained. As the acceleration increases, the mutual information in a small interdetector separation first increases and then decreases. For an intermediate interdetector separation, the mutual information may oscillate with the increase of acceleration. For a not large interdetector separation, when we take large acceleration and small radius, as the energy gap increases, the mutual information first decreases, then oscillates, and finally goes to zero. The combination of large acceleration and small radius corresponds to the fast rotation, which significantly modifies the vacuum fluctuations of the field, leading to the oscillatory behavior. Furthermore, the oscillation intensifies near the boundary, which indicates that it is related to the coherent superposition of boundary reflections.

</details>


### [138] [The Impact of Qubit Connectivity on Quantum Advantage in Noisy IQP Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12635)
*Leonardo Placidi,Enrico Rinaldi,Keisuke Fujii,Chen-Yu Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了量子比特连接性对IQP电路在噪声量子硬件上经典可模拟性边界的影响，发现稀疏连接架构由于需要额外的路由操作而增加编译深度，从而降低了保持量子优势所需的噪声容限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IQP电路被认为是展示近期量子优势的候选方案，但在噪声实现中，一旦电路深度超过噪声相关的临界阈值，其经典计算硬度就会消失。量子比特的连接性是一个关键参数，因为稀疏架构需要额外的路由来实现长程相互作用，从而增加了编译后的电路深度。

Method: 提出了连接性感知的编译IQP电路分析框架。结合稀疏几何结构（包括二维网格）的解析深度估计，以及在七个基于公开拓扑的硬件接地实验设备模型上进行原生门集感知的编译实验。通过报告的双量子比特门错误率来近似有效噪声水平，比较稀疏和全连接架构在相同噪声IQP可模拟性边界位置下能容忍的有效噪声量。

Result: 结果表明，稀疏连接性需要更低的有效噪声水平来维持相对于噪声IQP可模拟性边界的相同裕度。稀疏架构相对于全连接架构在保持量子优势方面需要更严格的噪声要求。

Conclusion: 研究提供了一个量化框架，用于确定编译后的IQP实验何时可能保持在经典可模拟范围之外，或进入经典可模拟范围。连接性是影响噪声量子硬件上IQP电路可模拟性边界的关键因素，稀疏架构由于路由开销而降低了噪声容限。

Abstract: Instantaneous Quantum Polynomial-time (IQP) circuits are a candidate for demonstrating near-term quantum advantage, as their sampling task is believed to be classically hard in the ideal theoretical setting under standard complexity-theoretic assumptions. In noisy implementations, however, this hardness can disappear once circuit depth exceeds a noise-dependent critical threshold. We show that qubit connectivity is a key parameter in this transition, since sparse architectures require additional routing to implement long-range interactions, thereby increasing compiled circuit depth. To make this explicit, we present a connectivity-aware analysis of compiled IQP circuits. For a fixed abstract IQP instance, different hardware connectivity graphs yield different compiled depths and thus different effective positions relative to the noisy-IQP simulatability boundary. We quantify this architecture-dependent shift using the compiled depth overhead and the corresponding simulatability margin. We combine analytic depth estimates for sparse geometries, including the two-dimensional grid, with native-gateset-aware compilation experiments across seven hardware-grounded experimental device models derived from publicly available topologies. To compare these device models under a unified empirical framework, we approximate the effective noise level primarily through reported two-qubit gate error rates. This lets us compare how much effective noise sparse and fully connected architectures can tolerate for the same position relative to the noisy-IQP simulatability boundary. Our results show that sparse connectivity requires a lower effective noise level to sustain the same margin relative to the noisy-IQP simulatability boundary, and they provide a quantitative framework for determining when compiled IQP experiments are likely to remain outside, or instead enter, the classically simulatable regime.

</details>


### [139] [Interferometrically Enhanced Asymmetry in Strong-field Ionization with Bright Squeezed Vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12646)
*G. Singh,T. Rook,J. Rivera-Dean,C. Figueira de Morisson Faria*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子光统计可用于控制强场电离的隧穿过程，使用双色线性偏振场（强相干驱动+弱亮压缩真空）可使光电子动量分布不对称性比经典场增强数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用量子光统计特性来控制强场电离过程，特别是隧穿步骤，以超越经典光场的控制能力。

Method: 采用双色线性偏振场：强相干驱动场与弱亮压缩真空（BSV）场组合，通过模拟研究光电子动量分布（PMDs），并使用基于强场近似（SFA）的半经典分析方法。

Result: 光电子动量分布的不对称性比强度相当的经典场增强了数个数量级，这种增强与BSV场的非经典统计特性直接相关，瞬时场振幅的波动显著改变了隧穿电离概率。

Conclusion: 量子光统计为强场电离的隧穿步骤提供了选择性控制，可用于重建电离路径并从强场可观测量中提取亚周期动力学信息。

Abstract: We demonstrate that quantum light statistics can be used to control strong-field ionization at the tunneling step. Using a bichromatic linearly polarized field composed of a strong coherent driver and a weak bright squeezed vacuum (BSV), we show through simulation that photoelectron momentum distributions (PMDs) exhibit asymmetries that exceed those obtained with classical fields of comparable intensity by orders of magnitude. This enhancement is uniquely linked to the nonclassical statistics of the BSV field. A semiclassical analysis based on the strong-field approximation (SFA) reveals that the effect originates from fluctuations in the instantaneous field amplitude, which strongly modify the tunneling ionization probability while leaving the electron's continuum dynamics essentially unchanged. This selective control enables reconstruction of ionization pathways and provides a robust route to extract sub-cycle dynamics from strong-field observables.

</details>


### [140] [Restoring polarization entanglement from solid-state photon sources by time-dependent photonic control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12661)
*Ismail Nassar,Dan Cogan,Ido Schwartz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种光子补偿协议，通过时间相关的相干操作直接去除发射器引起的相位演化，恢复偏振纠缠，无需时间后选择


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子发射器中相干内部动力学（如能级分裂）会在发射光上施加确定性时间相关相位。当发射时间随机且探测器时间分辨率有限时，这种相位演化变得无法解析，从而抑制可观测的纠缠。

Method: 采用光子补偿协议，对发射的光子应用同步的时间相关相干操作，独立于发射时间地反转累积相位。使用半导体量子点中的激子精细结构分裂作为模型系统，实现动态相位调制并进行时间分辨双光子偏振层析。

Result: 恢复了平稳的双光子偏振态，恢复了偏振纠缠，无需时间后选择且独立于探测器时间分辨率。

Conclusion: 该方法为稳健的固态纠缠光子源提供了可扩展途径，并建立了在集成平台中去除相干发射器动力学对光子纠缠影响的策略。

Abstract: Quantum states of light are central resources for quantum communication, networking, and photonic information processing. In many quantum emitters, coherent internal dynamics arising from intrinsic or field-induced level splittings imprint a deterministic, time-dependent phase on the emitted light. When emission times are stochastic and detector timing resolution is finite, this phase evolution becomes effectively unresolved, suppressing observable entanglement.
  Here, we demonstrate a photonic-compensation protocol that removes this emitter-induced phase evolution directly in the photonic domain. Rather than modifying the emitter, we apply synchronized, time-dependent coherent operations to the emitted photons that reverse the accumulated phase independently of the emission time. Using exciton fine-structure splitting in a semiconductor quantum dot as a model system, we implement dynamic phase modulation and perform time-resolved two-photon polarization tomography. We show that this restores a stationary two-photon polarization state and recovers polarization entanglement without temporal post-selection and independently of detector timing resolution.
  Our approach provides a scalable route to robust solid-state entangled-photon sources and, more broadly, establishes a strategy for removing the imprint of coherent emitter dynamics on photonic entanglement in integrated platforms.

</details>


### [141] [Quantum analogues of exponential sensitivity: from Loschmidt echo to Krylov complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12707)
*Ignacio García-Mata,Diego A. Wisnicki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对量子系统中捕捉经典混沌行为的三种替代量进行了教学性概述：Loschmidt回波、无序时序关联函数和Krylov复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于量子力学的幺正性，经典混沌中的指数敏感性在量子系统中难以直接体现，因此需要寻找能够捕捉类似行为的替代量。

Method: 通过教学性概述的方式，系统介绍三种近年来研究重点的量子混沌表征量：Loschmidt回波、无序时序关联函数和Krylov复杂度。

Result: 提供了三种量子混沌表征量的全面教学介绍，为理解量子系统中混沌行为的量化方法建立了框架。

Conclusion: Loschmidt回波、无序时序关联函数和Krylov复杂度是研究量子混沌的重要工具，它们为理解量子系统中的混沌行为提供了有效的替代方案。

Abstract: One of the fundamental manifestations of classical chaos is exponential sensitivity to initial conditions that is, two trajectories starting from nearly identical initial states diverge exponentially over time. This behavior is quantified by the Lyapunov exponents. Due to the unitary nature of quantum mechanics, such exponential divergence is elusive in quantum systems. As a result, several alternative quantities have been proposed and studied in recent years to capture analogous behavior.
  In this article, we present a pedagogical overview of three such quantities that have been the focus of intense research in recent years: the Loschmidt echo, out-of-time-order correlators (OTOCs), and Krylov complexity.

</details>


### [142] [Testing the 3D QRNG by Undoing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12724)
*J. M. Agüero Trejo,Cristian S. Calude,O. C. Stoica*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种测试光子3D QRNG是否按理论工作的方法，通过撤销QRNG实现的幺正演化来验证其生成高度不可计算/不可预测随机序列的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要验证量子随机数生成器是否真正按照理论工作，确保其生成的随机序列具有理论证明的强不可计算性和不可预测性，同时排除量子测量精度限制等潜在问题。

Method: 通过撤销3D QRNG实现的幺正演化来进行测试，该方法可以验证幺正性、噪声大小、光子损失、系统性和可重复的制造误差等潜在错误。

Result: 测试能够确认3D QRNG的理论特征（如强不可计算性和不可预测性），或在必要时提供修正指导，同时确保QRNG不受Wigner-Araki-Yanase定理描述的量子测量精度限制影响。

Conclusion: 该方法可轻松集成到QRNG中，作为实验认证手段，为光子3D量子随机数生成器的可靠性和安全性提供验证框架。

Abstract: We propose a method to test whether a photonic 3D QRNG works according to the underlying theory, thereby generating highly incomputable/unpredictable sequences of random digits. The test relies on undoing the unitary evolution realized by the 3D QRNG. The test verifies the unitarity, the magnitude of the noise, and other potential errors, such as photon loss or systematic and reproducible fabrication errors. Therefore, the test can confirm the theoretically proven features of the 3D QRNG, such as strong incomputability and unpredictability, or how one has to correct it, if necessary. In addition, the test ensures that the QRNG is not affected by limits of quantum measurement accuracy, as those described in the Wigner-Araki-Yanase Theorem. The test can be easily incorporated into the QRNG and used as a means of experimental certification.

</details>


### [143] [Quantum Chaos in Phase Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12741)
*Martina Hentschel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了介观器件作为量子混沌行为的模型系统，重点研究了经典-量子对应原理在相空间中的应用，以更全面地理解系统动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 介观器件（尺寸在几个到几十个波长范围内）是观察量子混沌行为的典型模型系统。这些电子和光子台球腔足够小，使得干涉效应不可忽略，但经典的射线或粒子追踪图像仍能提供对系统动力学的重要理解。研究旨在扩展经典-量子对应原理的应用范围，从实空间扩展到相空间，以获得对动力学行为更深入全面的理解。

Method: 采用半经典概念，将经典-量子（或射线-波）对应原理应用于介观器件。特别强调将这一原理从实空间扩展到相空间，使得位置和动量信息都能贡献于对动力学行为的理解。通过电子和光子台球腔作为模型系统进行研究。

Result: 研究发现，将经典-量子对应原理扩展到相空间特别有用，能够提供对系统动力学行为更深入和更全面的理解。相空间方法允许同时考虑位置和动量信息，这在理解介观器件的量子混沌行为方面具有重要价值。

Conclusion: 介观器件作为量子混沌行为的模型系统，通过将经典-量子对应原理从实空间扩展到相空间，可以获得对系统动力学更全面的理解。这种相空间方法结合了位置和动量信息，为理解介观尺度下的量子混沌现象提供了更强大的理论框架。

Abstract: Mesoscopic devices, with system sizes in the range of several to several dozens wavelengths, represent paradigmatic model systems for the observation of quantum chaotic behaviour based on semiclassical concepts. Those electronic and photonic billiard cavities are small enough for interference effects not to be ignored. Nonetheless, the classical ray or particle tracing picture can often provide a substantial understanding of the dynamics of the system along the lines of classical-quantum, or ray-wave correspondence. This well-established principle turns out to be particularly useful when applied not only in real space, but by extending it to phase space such that both location and momentum information can contribute to a deeper and more comprehensive understanding of the dynamical behaviour.

</details>


### [144] [Quantum chaos in many-body systems of indistinguishable particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12745)
*Juan-Diego Urbina,Klaus Richter*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文综述了量子多体系统中半经典方法的最新进展，特别是从传统单粒子系统扩展到N粒子量子场系统的半经典理论，重点关注多体量子干涉效应及其在量子混沌现象中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统半经典方法主要处理单粒子系统在ħ→0极限下的经典对应，但缺乏对多体量子系统（N个全同粒子）在ħ_eff=1/N→0极限下的理论框架。需要建立能够描述多体量子干涉效应的半经典理论，以理解多体系统中的量子混沌现象。

Method: 基于van Vleck-Gutzwiller半经典传播子的多体版本，建立了多体半经典理论框架。该方法将传统相空间结构分析扩展到多体系统，考虑全同粒子的不可区分性导致的量子干涉效应，特别是多体量子干涉。

Result: 该理论为理解多体量子混沌现象提供了统一框架：包括多体系统的随机矩阵谱关联、多体本征态的普适形态、类似介观弱局域化的干涉效应，以及表征多体关联扩散的时序外关联函数。

Conclusion: 多体半经典理论成功地将传统半经典方法扩展到量子场系统，为理解多体量子干涉和量子混沌现象提供了理论基础，建立了相空间结构与多体量子性质之间的关键联系。

Abstract: In quantum systems with a classical limit, advanced semiclassical methods provide the crucial link between phase-space structures, reflecting the distinction between chaotic, mixed or integrable classical dynamics, and the corresponding quantum properties. Well established techniques dealing with ergodic wave interference in the usual semiclassical limit $\hbar \to 0$, where the classical limit is given by Hamiltonian mechanics of particles, constitute a now standard part of the toolkit of theoretical physics. During the last years, these ideas have been extended into the field theoretical domain of systems composed of $N$ indistinguishable particles, aka quantum fields, displaying a different type of semiclassical limit $\hbar_{\rm eff}=1/N \to 0$ and accounting for genuine many-body quantum interference. The foundational concept behind this idea of many-body interference, the many-body version of the van Vleck-Gutzwillers semiclassical propagator, is explained in detail. Based on this the corresponding semiclassical many-body theory is reviewed. It provides a unified framework for understanding a variety of quantum chaotic phenomena addressed, including random-matrix spectral correlations in many-body systems, the universal morphology of many-body eigenstates, interference effects kin to mesoscopic weak localization, and the key to the scrambling of many-body correlations characterized by out-of-time-order correlators.

</details>


### [145] [Distinguishability of locally diagonal orthogonally invariant quantum states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12808)
*Nathaniel Johnston,Vincent Russo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在LOCC、可分离和PPT测量下量子态的可区分性，重点关注局部对角正交不变(LDOI)态，证明了最优PPT和可分离测量可以取为LDOI形式，LOCC上确界可由LDOI LOCC POVM逼近，将优化问题维度从n⁴降至O(n²)，并为正交LDOI基的可区分性建立了高效可计算界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子态在不同测量类别下的可区分性是一个基础问题，但通常涉及高维优化。LDOI态包含许多重要量子态族，研究其可区分性能简化优化问题并揭示不同测量类别之间的关系。

Method: 通过局部对角正交旋转不变性定义LDOI态类，证明最优PPT和可分离测量可限制为LDOI形式，利用对称性将优化问题维度从n⁴降至O(n²)，为LDOI基的可区分性建立高效可计算界限，并分析LOCC、可分离和PPT测量之间的差距。

Result: 证明了对于LDOI态，最优PPT和可分离测量可限制为LDOI形式；LOCC上确界可由LDOI LOCC POVM逼近；将优化维度从n⁴降至O(n²)；对于一大类正交LDOI基（包括所有两量子比特情况），LOCC上确界等于PPT和可分离最优值；PPT和LOCC可区分性之间的差距最多为(n-2)/(2n²)。

Conclusion: LDOI态的对称性显著简化了量子态可区分性分析，使得高效计算界限成为可能，并为不同测量类别之间的关系提供了深入理解，特别是在两量子比特情况下LOCC、可分离和PPT测量达到相同最优值。

Abstract: We study the distinguishability of quantum states under local operations with classical communication (LOCC), separable, and positive-partial-transpose (PPT) measurements, focusing on locally diagonal orthogonally invariant (LDOI) states -- those invariant under local diagonal orthogonal twirling. This class includes many important families such as Werner states, isotropic states, X-states, and Dicke states. We show that optimal PPT and separable measurements for distinguishing LDOI states can always be taken to be LDOI, and the LOCC supremum can be approached by LDOI LOCC POVMs, enabling a dimensional reduction from $n^4$ to $O(n^2)$ in the associated optimization problems. We establish efficiently computable bounds on the distinguishability of orthonormal LDOI bases and prove that for a broad class of such bases -- including all two-qubit cases -- the LOCC supremum equals the PPT and separable optima. More generally, we show the gap between PPT and LOCC distinguishability is at most $(n-2)/(2n^2)$ for local dimension $n$.

</details>


### [146] [The use of the output states generated by the broadcasting of entanglement in quantum teleportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12823)
*Iulia Ghiu,Catalina Cirneci,George Alexandru Nemnes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文发现了量子隐形传态最大保真度与作为量子信道的不可分X态并发度之间的关系定理，证明了非局域广播纠缠产生的输出态具有更高的并发度，且所有通过局域和非局域非对称广播纠缠产生的不可分态都可用于量子隐形传态，其中非局域广播方案能获得更高的最大保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子隐形传态性能与量子信道纠缠度量之间的关系，探索通过纠缠广播方案产生的输出态作为量子信道的适用性，比较不同广播方案对隐形传态性能的影响。

Method: 建立量子隐形传态最大保真度与不可分X态并发度之间的数学定理关系；计算局域和非局域非对称广播纠缠产生的输出态的并发度；利用该定理分析这些输出态作为量子信道在隐形传态中的适用性；比较两种广播方案下的最大保真度。

Result: 证明了并发度在非局域广播情况下更高；所有通过局域和非局域非对称广播纠缠产生的不可分态都可用于量子隐形传态；非局域广播方案产生的量子信道能获得更高的最大保真度。

Conclusion: 量子隐形传态性能与量子信道的并发度直接相关，非局域广播纠缠方案能产生更高质量的量子信道，为优化量子通信协议提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In this article, we find a theorem that gives a relation between the maximal fidelity of teleportation and the concurrence of the inseparable $X$ state used as a quantum channel in this process. Furthermore, we evaluate the concurrence of the output states generated by the local and nonlocal asymmetric broadcasting of entanglement and prove that the concurrence is greater in the case of nonlocal broadcasting. We analyze the possibility of using the output states obtained by the broadcasting of entanglement as quantum channels in quantum teleportation. We prove, with the help of the above-mentioned theorem, that all the inseparable states given by the local and nonlocal asymmetric broadcasting of entanglement are useful for quantum teleportation. Finally, we show that the maximal fidelity of teleportation is greater in the case when the second scenario is used, i.e., when the quantum channel is generated by the nonlocal asymmetric broadcasting of entanglement.

</details>


### [147] [2D quantum-path interference in high-harmonic generation driven by highly-bichromatic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12838)
*Xiaozhou Zou,Lucie Jurkovičová,Anne Weber,Cong Zhao,Martin Albrecht,Ondřej Finke,Alexandr Vendl,Annika Grenfell,Wojciech Szuba,Jaroslav Nejdl,Eric Constant,Margarita Khokhlova,Emilio Pisanty,Ondřej Hort,Amelle Zaïr*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 实验观测到高次谐波产生中的新型二维量子路径干涉现象，由正交偏振双色场驱动，为阿秒电子动力学光谱学提供新途径


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高次谐波产生中量子路径干涉的新维度，通过正交偏振双色场实现二维量子路径干涉，为研究阿秒电子动力学提供更高维度的光谱学工具

Method: 使用正交偏振双色场驱动高次谐波产生，通过调节两色场的相对相位测量谐波强度调制；采用强场近似和鞍点法计算分析多量子轨道贡献

Result: 观测到二维量子路径干涉现象：奇次谐波强度调制呈现单峰结构，偶次谐波呈现双峰结构；偶极响应继承了正交偏振驱动场的动态对称性

Conclusion: 二维量子路径干涉为高次谐波光谱学提供了新维度，能够提升量子路径干涉的维度，为阿秒电子动力学研究开辟新途径

Abstract: We experimentally observe a new type of quantum-path interference, in two-dimensional(2D-QPI), in high-harmonic generation (HHG) driven by an orthogonally-polarised highly-bichromatic field. This regime is marked by comparable intensities of the two orthogonal colours. In this highly-bichromatic regime, we demonstrate that 2D-QPI is encoded in the measured harmonic intensity modulations with respect to the relative phase of the two-colour field. The modulations of the odd-order harmonics show a monomodal behaviour, whereas the even harmonics are modulated in a bimodal structure. Our calculations using the strong-field approximation and saddle-point method disentangle contributions from multiple quantum orbits in this HHG regime, revealing that the dipole response for both odd and even harmonics inherits the dynamic symmetry of the orthogonally-polarised driving field. This new type of 2D-QPI offers a novel route to HHG spectroscopy of attosecond electron dynamics by lifting up the dimensionality of the quantum paths involved in the interference.

</details>


### [148] [Fast and accurate AI-based pre-decoders for surface codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12841)
*Christopher Chamberland,Jan Olle,Muyuan Li,Scott Thornton,Igor Baratta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于AI的局部预解码器架构，用于表面码的快速并行解码，结合全局解码器实现亚微秒级解码时间，并引入噪声学习架构直接从实验数据估计解码权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时容错量子计算需要快速、可扩展的解码架构。传统全局解码方法在大型表面码上计算开销大，难以满足实时性要求。需要开发并行、高效的解码方案来降低解码延迟，同时保持或提高逻辑错误率性能。

Method: 1. 引入基于AI的预解码器，在局部并行执行错误校正，消除大部分物理错误后将剩余综合征传递给下游全局解码器。2. 模块化架构与任意表面码全局解码算法兼容。3. 实现完全开源。4. 引入噪声学习架构，直接从实验可访问的综合征统计数据推断解码权重，无需显式电路级噪声模型。

Result: 1. 与uncorrelated PyMatching集成后，在NVIDIA GB300 GPU上实现每轮亚微秒级（O(1μs)）端到端解码时间。2. 多GPU并行解码可将时间降至远低于O(1μs)/轮。3. 训练更大模型可进一步降低逻辑错误率，在距离-13内优于correlated PyMatching。4. 纯数据驱动的图权重估计性能接近uncorrelated PyMatching，在某些情况下超过correlated PyMatching。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了实用、模块化、高吞吐量的解码框架，适用于大规模表面码实现。预解码器架构显著降低解码延迟，噪声学习架构在硬件噪声模型未知或时变时仍能实现优化解码，为实时容错量子计算提供了关键解码解决方案。

Abstract: Fast, scalable decoding architectures that operate in a block-wise parallel fashion across space and time are essential for real-time fault-tolerant quantum computing. We introduce a scalable AI-based pre-decoder for the surface code that performs local, parallel error correction with low decoding runtimes, removing the majority of physical errors before passing residual syndromes to a downstream global decoder. This modular architecture is backend-agnostic and composes with arbitrary global decoding algorithms designed for surface codes, and our implementation is completely open source. Integrated with uncorrelated PyMatching, the pipeline achieves end-to-end decoding runtimes of order $\mathcal{O}(1 μ\text{s})$ per round at large code distances on NVIDIA GB300 GPUs while reducing logical error rates (LERs) relative to global decoding alone. In a block-wise parallel decoding scheme with access to multiple GPUs, the decoding runtime can be reduced to well below $\mathcal{O}(1 μ\text{s})$ per round. We observe further LER improvements by training a larger model, outperforming correlated PyMatching up to distance-13. We additionally introduce a noise-learning architecture that infers decoding weights directly from experimentally accessible syndrome statistics without requiring an explicit circuit-level noise model. We show that purely data-driven graph weight estimation can nearly match uncorrelated PyMatching and exceed correlated PyMatching in certain regimes, enabling highly-optimized decoding when hardware noise models are unknown or time-varying, as well as training pre-decoders with realistic noise models. Together, these results establish a practical, modular, and high-throughput decoding framework suitable for large-distance surface-code implementations.

</details>


### [149] [Hamiltonian Chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12976)
*Steven Tomsovic*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本章综述了哈密顿混沌与量子混沌的联系，重点介绍了混沌研究的理论工具、几何特性、扰动响应及复化动力学，强调直观解释与图示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过半经典方法，量子混沌研究需要依赖哈密顿混沌理论。本章旨在展示哈密顿混沌中的关键主题如何直接关联到各种量子混沌研究问题，为理解量子混沌提供经典理论基础。

Method: 采用综述性方法，首先介绍混沌研究的理论和计算工具（如截面法、混沌范式、稳定性分析、符号动力学），然后讨论混沌的几何特性、混沌系统对扰动的响应，以及哈密顿动力学的复化。

Result: 建立了哈密顿混沌与量子混沌研究之间的直接联系，提供了理解量子混沌现象的经典框架，强调了直观解释和图示在理解复杂混沌概念中的重要性。

Conclusion: 哈密顿混沌研究为量子混沌提供了重要的理论基础和工具，两者通过半经典方法紧密相连。本章通过直观解释和图示展示了这种联系，为相关研究提供了参考框架。

Abstract: Through semiclassical methods the subject of quantum chaos motivates and depends on Hamiltonian chaos research. Presented here is a selection of Hamiltonian chaos topics that in this way get directly related to any of a variety of quantum chaos research problems. The chapter begins with a description of various useful theoretical and computational tools of chaos research, e.g.~surfaces of section, paradigms of chaos, stability analysis, and symbolic dynamics... This is followed by discussions regarding the geometry of chaos, how chaotic systems respond to perturbations, and the complexification of Hamiltonian dynamics. The emphasis is on intuitive explanations and illustrations of various ideas with the references containing more mathematically rigorous expositions.

</details>


### [150] [Classical and Quantum Speedups for Non-Convex Optimization via Energy Conserving Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13022)
*Yihang Sun,Huaijin Wang,Patrick Hayden,Jose Blanchet*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文首次对能量守恒下降(ECD)算法进行理论分析，证明其在双阱目标函数中相比梯度下降方法能实现指数级加速，并提出了随机ECD和量子ECD变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECD算法作为全局非凸优化方法，能够逃离严格局部极小值并收敛到全局最小值，这使其在机器学习优化中具有吸引力。然而，缺乏对该算法的理论分析，需要建立其理论基础并探索量子版本的可能性。

Method: 1. 在一维设置下形式化随机ECD动力学(sECD)，引入能量保持噪声；2. 提出量子ECD哈密顿量(qECD)，为量子算法提供基础；3. 针对正双阱目标函数，计算从局部极小值到全局极小值的期望命中时间；4. 比较sECD和qECD与各自梯度下降基线的性能。

Result: 1. 证明sECD和qECD相比各自的梯度下降基线（随机梯度下降及其量子化）都能实现指数级加速；2. 对于具有高势垒的目标函数，qECD相比sECD能实现进一步的加速；3. 为量子优化算法提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: 该研究首次为ECD算法提供了理论分析框架，证明了其在非凸优化中的优越性能，特别是量子版本在特定问题上的进一步加速潜力，为开发更高效的全局优化算法奠定了基础。

Abstract: The Energy Conserving Descent (ECD) algorithm was recently proposed (De Luca & Silverstein, 2022) as a global non-convex optimization method. Unlike gradient descent, appropriately configured ECD dynamics escape strict local minima and converge to a global minimum, making it appealing for machine learning optimization.
  We present the first analytical study of ECD, focusing on the one-dimensional setting for this first installment. We formalize a stochastic ECD dynamics (sECD) with energy-preserving noise, as well as a quantum analog of the ECD Hamiltonian (qECD), providing the foundation for a quantum algorithm through Hamiltonian simulation.
  For positive double-well objectives, we compute the expected hitting time from a local to the global minimum. We prove that both sECD and qECD yield exponential speedup over respective gradient descent baselines--stochastic gradient descent and its quantization. For objectives with tall barriers, qECD achieves a further speedup over sECD.

</details>


### [151] [Floquet Many-Body Cages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13027)
*Tom Ben-Ami,Roderich Moessner,Markus Heyl*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种在Floquet电路中工程化多体笼的新方法，用于实现非平衡量子态，特别是在量子硬盘模型中构造具有拓扑性质和π-准能模的Floquet多体笼。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体笼作为量子物质中非遍历行为的新途径，但如何工程化新型多体笼以实现新颖的非平衡量子态仍是一个挑战。论文旨在开发一种通用策略来设计和结构化Floquet多体笼。

Method: 首先识别了能够承载多体笼的Floquet电路的显式通用构造，然后提出了工程化和结构化Floquet多体笼的通用策略。以量子硬盘模型作为通用约束模型系统进行演示，该模型可在里德堡原子阵列中实现。

Result: 成功构造了具有拓扑性质和π-准能模的Floquet多体笼，这意味着"时间晶体"时空序的存在。所提出的方案可直接扩展到一般量子电路。

Conclusion: 开发了一种工程化Floquet多体笼的新工具，为驱动系统中非平衡行为的工程设计提供了新途径，特别是在实现具有拓扑特性和时间晶体序的非平衡量子态方面。

Abstract: Many-body cages have very recently emerged as a general route for nonergodic behaviour in quantum matter. Here, we show that new types of many-body cages can be engineered in Floquet circuits with the potential to realize novel nonequilibrium quantum states. For that purpose, we first identify an explicit, general construction of Floquet circuits capable of hosting many-body cages. We then present a generic strategy to engineer and structure Floquet many-body cages. We demonstrate the developed scheme for the quantum hard disk model as a generic constrained model system, realizable for instance in Rydberg atom arrays. We construct Floquet circuits yielding Floquet many-body cages with topological properties and $π$-quasienergy modes, implying `time crystalline' spatiotemporal order. Our results can be directly extended to general quantum circuits, thus providing a new tool to engineer nonequilibrium behaviour in driven systems.

</details>


### [152] [Zeno Blockade Enabling Photonic Quantum Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13032)
*Mohammad-Ali Miri,Uchenna Chukwu,Nicholas Chancellor*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于非线性光学（和频生成/双光子吸收）的光学量子优化器实现方案，利用芝诺效应施加独立性约束，通过线性协议寻找带权重的最大独立集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用非线性光学实现光学量子优化器的潜力，将熵计算范式或量子退火与芝诺约束子空间相结合，为量子优化问题提供物理实现方案。

Method: 采用非线性光学（和频生成和/或双光子吸收）实现光学量子优化器，利用芝诺效应施加独立性约束，通过线性协议寻找带权重的最大独立集。该方法可视为熵计算范式（使用实时间演化而非虚时间演化）的实现，或视为芝诺约束子空间内的量子退火。

Result: 数值研究了协议各方面性能，包括相干与非相干芝诺效应的效果对比，发现相干芝诺效应具有更优性能。讨论了设备构建方案及误差缓解（特别是光子损失误差）的考虑因素。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了利用非线性光学实现光学量子优化器的可行性，通过芝诺效应施加约束并结合线性协议，能够有效解决带权重的最大独立集问题，相干芝诺效应相比非相干版本表现出更好的性能。

Abstract: In this work we explore the potential of implementing an optical quantum optimizer using non-linear optics, specifically using sum-frequency generation and/or two photon absorption. This proposal uses Zeno effects to enforce independence constraints and then a linear protocol to find a maximum independent set in a way where the elements of the set can be weighted. Our proposal can either be viewed as an implementation of the entropy computing paradigm presented in [Nguyen et.~al.~Communications Physics 1, 411, 8] which uses real rather than imaginary time evolution, or as quantum annealing within a Zeno constrained subspace. We discuss how such a device could be built, and considerations such as error mitigation, particularly for photon-loss errors. We numerically study aspects of the protocol, including the effect of coherent versus incoherent incarnations of the Zeno effect, finding superior performance from the former.

</details>
